ICH M11 elements to USDM v4 (usdm-rdf), as stated in two published mappings

https://w3id.org/m11-rdf/m11-usdm.sssom.tsv

A mapping set in SSSOM format: 496 records. This page shows the file as it is: the set metadata from its header, then one table row per record, columns in file order. Links are added to record_id, subject_id and object_id, expanded with the set's own prefixes.

Files: m11-usdm.sssom.tsv · m11-usdm.sssom.ttl

Mapping set

mapping_set_id
https://w3id.org/m11-rdf/m11-usdm.sssom.tsv
mapping_set_version
0.4.0
mapping_set_title
ICH M11 elements to USDM v4 (usdm-rdf), as stated in two published mappings
mapping_set_description
One record per row of the HL7 FHIR Clinical Study Protocol IG ballot mapping (May 2026) and of the CDISC DDF-RA v4.0.0 M11 mapping whose M11 side is identified against m11-rdf and whose USDM target resolves in usdm-rdf (USDM v4.0.0). Neither mapping is primary. The link from element to USDM target is the publisher's; m11-rdf adds only the identification of the M11 side: by code (the row's NCIt Code is the concept of one element, or the row's section decides between namespaces), or by the row's element name when its normalised form (case, punctuation, brackets, white space) equals the term of exactly one Technical Specification element block. Target and condition paths as given, line breaks replaced by a space. Rows without a record are listed in reports/d2-residue.csv. Mapping statements contain content based on DDF-RA (https://github.com/cdisc-org/DDF-RA), used under CC BY 4.0, as published in CDISC DDF-RA v4.0.0 and in the HL7 FHIR Clinical Study Protocol IG ballot (May 2026, CC0); changes made: the M11 side identified against m11-rdf, the USDM side resolved against usdm-rdf. Draft, not normative; not an ICH, CDISC or HL7 artifact.
